소스 모음

화면과 빌드 소스를 파일 순서대로 이어 두었습니다. 주석은 코드 옆에 그대로 있습니다. data/ 과제 코드, 압축된 라이브러리, 빌드가 만든 data.js, hero-images.js, sources JSON은 넣지 않았습니다.

cover_letter/js/script.js

 1  /**
 2   * 자기소개서 페이지에서 현재 보고 있는 절에 맞춰 사이드 메뉴를 표시하고, 맨 위로 버튼을 연결한다.
 3   */
 4  (function () {
 5    "use strict";
 6  
 7    /**
 8     * data-section이 id와 같은 메뉴 링크에만 활성 클래스를 붙인다.
 9     * @param {string} id 현재 화면에 들어온 절의 id
10     */
11    function setActiveNav(id) {
12      document.querySelectorAll(".nav-link").forEach(function (link) {
13        var sec = link.getAttribute("data-section");
14        if (sec === id) {
15          link.classList.add("is-active");
16        } else {
17          link.classList.remove("is-active");
18        }
19      });
20    }
21  
22    /**
23     * 본문 절이 화면의 일정 비율 이상 보이면 해당 메뉴를 활성으로 바꾼다.
24     * 관찰이 시작되기 전에는 첫 절을 활성으로 둔다.
25     */
26    function initScrollSpy() {
27      var sections = document.querySelectorAll(".content-section[id]");
28      if (!sections.length) return;
29  
30      var observer = new IntersectionObserver(
31        function (entries) {
32          entries.forEach(function (entry) {
33            if (entry.isIntersecting && entry.intersectionRatio >= 0.25) {
34              setActiveNav(entry.target.id);
35            }
36          });
37        },
38        {
39          root: null,
40          rootMargin: "-20% 0px -55% 0px",
41          threshold: [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1]
42        }
43      );
44  
45      sections.forEach(function (section) {
46        observer.observe(section);
47      });
48  
49      /* 첫 화면 */
50      var first = sections[0];
51      if (first && first.id) setActiveNav(first.id);
52    }
53  
54    /**
55     * 맨 위로 버튼을 누르면 페이지 최상단으로 부드럽게 스크롤한다.
56     */
57    function initToTop() {
58      var btn = document.getElementById("toTop");
59      if (!btn) return;
60      btn.addEventListener("click", function () {
61        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
62      });
63    }
64  
65    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
66      initScrollSpy();
67      initToTop();
68    });
69  })();

cover_letter/profile.html

  1  <!-- 자기소개 본문 화면. 절마다 사이드 메뉴 활성 표시와 맨 위로 이동을 쓴다. -->
  2  <!DOCTYPE html>
  3  <html lang="ko" data-default-theme="light">
  4  <head>
  5    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  6    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  7    <title>김신욱 · 자기소개</title>
  8    <meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow, noarchive, nosnippet" />
  9    <meta name="tdm-reservation" content="1" />
 10    <meta name="referrer" content="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" />
 11    <script>
 12      (function () {
 13        try {
 14          var theme = localStorage.getItem("portfolio-theme-v2");
 15          if (theme === "dark" || theme === "light") {
 16            document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
 17          }
 18        } catch (e) {
 19          /* 테마 저장소를 사용할 수 없는 환경에서는 기본 테마를 사용합니다. */
 20        }
 21      })();
 22    </script>

119        <section id="about" class="content-section" aria-labelledby="heading-about">
120          <h2 id="heading-about" class="section-heading">
121            <span class="section-heading-text">소개</span>
122          </h2>
123          <div class="prose">
124            <p>
125              4년제 응용통계학과(SW응용통계학)를 졸업한 뒤 IT 분야를 꾸준히 학습해 왔고,
126              <a href="https://blog.naver.com/fifo23" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">코탐록 네이버 블로그</a>를 운영할 예정이며 취업 준비를 이어가고 있습니다.
127              웹·모바일 개발과 AI·IT 전반에 관심이 많습니다.
128            </p>
129          </div>
130        </section>

132        <section id="profile" class="content-section" aria-labelledby="heading-profile">
133          <h2 id="heading-profile" class="section-heading">
134            <span class="section-heading-text">프로필</span>
135          </h2>
136          <ul class="profile-list">
137            <li><span class="profile-k">성별</span><span class="profile-v">남성</span></li>
138            <li><span class="profile-k">나이</span><span class="profile-v">30대 후반</span></li>
139            <li><span class="profile-k">학력</span><span class="profile-v">대졸, 학점은행제(멀티미디어학) 도전 중</span></li>
140            <li><span class="profile-k">목표</span><span class="profile-v">취업(이력서·자소서), AI 서비스를 제대로 활용하기</span></li>
141            <li><span class="profile-k">취미</span><span class="profile-v">프로그래밍(주로 책 위주 학습)</span></li>
142            <li><span class="profile-k">온라인 학습</span><span class="profile-v">패스트캠퍼스, 코드잇, 인프런, 유데미, KMOOC, 경기지식 등</span></li>
143            <li><span class="profile-k">성장 과제</span><span class="profile-v">사교성, 도전정신 — 꾸준히 보완 중입니다.</span></li>
144          </ul>

209        <section id="motivation" class="content-section" aria-labelledby="heading-motivation">
210          <h2 id="heading-motivation" class="section-heading">
211            <span class="section-heading-text">지원 동기 및 포부</span>
212          </h2>
213          <div class="prose">
214            <p>
215              IT 관련 개발직을 목표로 하며, 웹·모바일뿐 아니라 다른 IT 기술과 AI에도 관심이 많습니다.
216              IT기술직·현장직, 웹·앱 개발자로 일하고 싶습니다.
217            </p>
218            <p>
219              최근 AI 기술에 집중해 학습하고 있으며, 백업·정리된 학습 노트와 프로젝트를 바탕으로 이력서와 자소서를 다듬어 지원할 계획입니다.
220              대학 졸업 이후 공백이 있었으나 하루 최소 약 3시간은 IT에 매진해 왔고, 일상에서도 IT 활용에 관심이 많습니다.
221            </p>
222            <p>
223              개인만으로는 어려운 서비스를 팀원과 협업해 만들고, 올바른 가이드라인을 지키며 프로젝트에 참여하고 싶습니다. IT 분야 취업 후 기술 발전에 기여하고자 합니다.
224            </p>
225          </div>
226        </section>
227  
228        <section id="motto" class="content-section" aria-labelledby="heading-motto">
229          <h2 id="heading-motto" class="section-heading">
230            <span class="section-heading-text">생활신조</span>
231          </h2>
232          <blockquote class="motto">
233            <p class="motto-line">필사즉생</p>
234            <p class="motto-sub">안 되는 것을 꾸준히 고민하며, 될 때까지 해내는 것이 제 신조입니다.</p>
235          </blockquote>
236        </section>

cv/js/ui.js

 1  /**
 2   * 이력서의 각 경력 항목에서 시작일·종료일을 읽어 재직 기간을 계산한다.
 3   * 종료일이 today이면 오늘 날짜로 바꾸고, 연·개월을 periodResult에 괄호로 표시한다.
 4   */
 5  function fillCareerPeriods() {
 6      $('.article').each(function () {
 7  
 8          const startDate = $(this).find('.startDate').text().trim();
 9  
10          if (startDate !== '') {
11  
12              const startDateArr = startDate.split('.').map(str => str.trim()).filter(Boolean);
13              let endDate = $(this).find('.endDate').text().trim();
14              let endDateArr;
15  
16              if (endDate === 'today') {
17  
18                  const now = new Date();
19                  const year = now.getFullYear();
20                  const month = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
21                  const day = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
22  
23                  endDateArr = [year, month, day];
24                  $(this).find('.endDate').text(`${year}. ${month}. ${day}.`);
25  
26              } else {
27  
28                  endDateArr = endDate.split('.').map(str => str.trim()).filter(Boolean);
29  
30                  const year = endDateArr[0];
31                  const month = String(parseInt(endDateArr[1], 10)).padStart(2, '0');
32                  const day = String(parseInt(endDateArr[2], 10)).padStart(2, '0');
33  
34                  endDateArr = [year, month, day];
35                  $(this).find('.endDate').text(`${year}. ${month}. ${day}.`);
36  
37              }
38  
39              const startDateCalc = new Date(startDateArr[0], startDateArr[1], startDateArr[2]);
40              const endDateCalc = new Date(endDateArr[0], endDateArr[1], endDateArr[2]);
41              const dateCalc = endDateCalc.getTime() - startDateCalc.getTime();
42              const dateCalcResult = dateCalc / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
43  
44              let dateResultText;
45              const dateResultYear = Math.floor(dateCalcResult / 365);
46              const dateResultMonth = Math.round((dateCalcResult - (dateResultYear * 365)) / 30);
47  
48              if (dateResultYear === 0 && dateResultMonth === 0) {
49                  dateResultText = '-';
50              } else if (dateResultYear === 0) {
51                  dateResultText = `${dateResultMonth}개월`;
52              } else if (dateResultMonth === 0) {
53                  dateResultText = `${dateResultYear}년`;
54              } else {
55                  dateResultText = `${dateResultYear}년 ${dateResultMonth}개월`;
56              }
57  
58              $(this).find('.periodResult').text(`(${dateResultText})`);
59          }
60  
61      });
62  }
63  
64  $(document).ready(fillCareerPeriods);

docs/build_code_reference.py

  1  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
  2  """소스 주석을 읽어 코드 설명 마크다운과 PDF를 만든다."""
  3  from __future__ import annotations
  4  
  5  import ast
  6  import html
  7  import os
  8  import re
  9  import shutil
 10  import subprocess
 11  import tempfile
 12  from pathlib import Path
 13  
 14  ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
 15  DOCS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
 16  MD_PATH = DOCS / "code-reference.md"
 17  HTML_PATH = DOCS / "code-reference.html"
 18  PDF_PATH = DOCS / "code-reference.pdf"
 19  
 20  ID_RE = re.compile(r"\d{7,8}")
 21  NAME_RE = re.compile("김신욱")
 22  
 23  
 24  def mask_privacy(text: str) -> str:
 25      """파일 목록에 섞인 학번과 이름을 가린다."""
 26      text = ID_RE.sub("OOOOOOOO", text)
 27      return NAME_RE.sub("OOO", text)
 28  
 29  
 30  def rel(path: Path) -> str:
 31      return path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
 32  
 33  
 34  class Doc:
 35      def __init__(self) -> None:
 36          self.blocks: list[tuple] = []
 37  
 38      def h1(self, text: str) -> None:
 39          self.blocks.append(("h1", text))
 40  
 41      def h2(self, text: str) -> None:
 42          self.blocks.append(("h2", text))
 43  
 44      def h3(self, text: str) -> None:
 45          self.blocks.append(("h3", text))
 46  
 47      def h4(self, text: str) -> None:
 48          self.blocks.append(("h4", text))
 49  
 50      def p(self, text: str) -> None:
 51          self.blocks.append(("p", text))
 52  
 53      def ul(self, items: list[str]) -> None:
 54          self.blocks.append(("ul", items))
 55  
 56      def code(self, text: str) -> None:
 57          self.blocks.append(("code", text))
 58  
 59      def render_md(self) -> str:
 60          out: list[str] = []
 61          for kind, payload in self.blocks:
 62              if kind == "h1":
 63                  out.append(f"# {payload}\n")
 64              elif kind == "h2":
 65                  out.append(f"\n## {payload}\n")
 66              elif kind == "h3":
 67                  out.append(f"\n### {payload}\n")
 68              elif kind == "h4":
 69                  out.append(f"\n#### {payload}\n")
 70              elif kind == "p":
 71                  out.append(payload + "\n")
 72              elif kind == "ul":
 73                  out.extend(f"- {item}" for item in payload)
 74                  out.append("")
 75              elif kind == "code":
 76                  out.append("```")
 77                  out.append(payload)
 78                  out.append("```\n")
 79          return "\n".join(out).strip() + "\n"
 80  
 81      def render_html(self) -> str:
 82          parts = [
 83              "<!DOCTYPE html>",
 84              '<html lang="ko">',
 85              "<head>",
 86              '<meta charset="utf-8" />',
 87              "<title>코드 설명</title>",
 88              "<style>",
 89              HTML_CSS,
 90              "</style>",
 91              "</head>",
 92              "<body>",
 93          ]
 94          first_h2 = True
 95          for kind, payload in self.blocks:
 96              if kind == "h1":
 97                  parts.append(f"<h1>{html.escape(payload)}</h1>")
 98              elif kind == "h2":
 99                  cls = "first" if first_h2 else ""
100                  first_h2 = False
101                  parts.append(f'<h2 class="{cls}">{html.escape(payload)}</h2>')
102              elif kind == "h3":
103                  parts.append(f"<h3>{html.escape(payload)}</h3>")
104              elif kind == "h4":
105                  parts.append(f"<h4>{html.escape(payload)}</h4>")
106              elif kind == "p":
107                  parts.append(f"<p>{inline_html(payload)}</p>")
108              elif kind == "ul":
109                  items = "".join(f"<li>{inline_html(item)}</li>" for item in payload)
110                  parts.append(f"<ul>{items}</ul>")
111              elif kind == "code":
112                  parts.append(f"<pre>{html.escape(payload)}</pre>")
113          parts.append("</body></html>")
114          return "\n".join(parts)
115  
116  
117  HTML_CSS = """
118  @page { margin: 18mm 16mm; }
119  body { font-family: "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif; font-size: 11pt; line-height: 1.55; color: #1c1c1c; }
120  h1 { font-size: 22pt; margin: 0 0 0.6em; }
121  h2 { font-size: 16pt; margin: 1.4em 0 0.4em; page-break-before: always; }
122  h2.first { page-break-before: auto; }
123  h3 { font-size: 13pt; margin: 1.1em 0 0.3em; page-break-after: avoid; }
124  h4 { font-size: 11.5pt; margin: 0.9em 0 0.25em; page-break-after: avoid; }
125  p, li { orphans: 3; widows: 3; }
126  ul { margin: 0.2em 0 0.8em; padding-left: 1.2em; }
127  code { font-family: Consolas, "Malgun Gothic", monospace; font-size: 9.5pt; }
128  pre { font-family: Consolas, "Malgun Gothic", monospace; font-size: 9.5pt; background: #f3f3f3;
129        padding: 8px 10px; white-space: pre-wrap; page-break-inside: avoid; }
130  """
131  
132  INLINE_CODE = re.compile(r"`([^`]+)`")
133  BOLD = re.compile(r"\*\*(.+?)\*\*")
134  
135  
136  def inline_html(text: str) -> str:
137      escaped = html.escape(text)
138      escaped = INLINE_CODE.sub(lambda m: f"<code>{m.group(1)}</code>", escaped)
139      escaped = BOLD.sub(lambda m: f"<strong>{m.group(1)}</strong>", escaped)
140      return escaped
141  
142  
143  def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
144      return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
145  
146  
147  def jsdoc_before(lines: list[str], index: int) -> str:
148      if index == 0 or lines[index - 1].strip() != "*/":
149          return ""
150      start = index - 1
151      while start >= 0 and "/**" not in lines[start]:
152          start -= 1
153      if start < 0:
154          return ""
155      chunk = "\n".join(lines[start:index])
156      body_lines = []
157      for line in chunk.splitlines():
158          line = re.sub(r"^\s*/?\*+/?\s?", "", line).strip()
159          if line in ("/**", "*/", "*"):
160              continue
161          if line:
162              body_lines.append(line)
163      return "\n".join(body_lines).strip()
164  
165  
166  def split_jsdoc(doc: str) -> tuple[str, list[str], str]:
167      desc: list[str] = []
168      params: list[str] = []
169      returns = ""
170      for line in doc.splitlines():
171          param = re.match(r"@param\s+\{([^}]+)\}\s+(\S+)\s*(.*)", line)
172          ret = re.match(r"@returns?\s+\{([^}]+)\}\s*(.*)", line)
173          if param:
174              extra = f" {param.group(3)}".rstrip()
175              params.append(f"{param.group(2)}: {param.group(1)}{extra}")
176          elif ret:
177              extra = f" {ret.group(2)}".rstrip()
178              returns = f"{ret.group(1)}{extra}"
179          elif not line.startswith("@"):
180              desc.append(line)
181      return " ".join(desc).strip(), params, returns
182  
183  
184  def clip_expr(text: str, limit: int = 72) -> str:
185      text = " ".join(text.split())
186      if len(text) <= limit:
187          return text
188      return text[: limit - 1] + "…"
189  
190  
191  def py_expr(node: ast.AST | None) -> str:
192      if node is None:
193          return ""
194      try:
195          return clip_expr(ast.unparse(node))
196      except Exception:
197          return "식"
198  
199  
200  def append_py_stmt(stmt: ast.AST, depth: int, lines: list[str], limit: int) -> None:
201      if len(lines) >= limit:
202          return
203      pad = "  " * depth
204      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr) and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant) and isinstance(stmt.value.value, str):
205          return
206      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass):
207          return
208      if isinstance(stmt, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)):
209          lines.append(f"{pad}{stmt.name} 을 정의한다")
210          return
211      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Return):
212          value = py_expr(stmt.value)
213          lines.append(f"{pad}반환 {value}" if value else f"{pad}반환")
214          return
215      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Assign):
216          targets = ", ".join(py_expr(target) for target in stmt.targets)
217          lines.append(f"{pad}{targets} ← {py_expr(stmt.value)}")
218          return
219      if isinstance(stmt, ast.AnnAssign):
220          if stmt.value is not None:
221              lines.append(f"{pad}{py_expr(stmt.target)} ← {py_expr(stmt.value)}")
222          else:
223              lines.append(f"{pad}필드 {py_expr(stmt.target)}")
224          return
225      if isinstance(stmt, ast.AugAssign):
226          lines.append(f"{pad}{py_expr(stmt.target)} ← {py_expr(stmt.target)} 연산 {py_expr(stmt.value)}")
227          return
228      if isinstance(stmt, ast.If):
229          lines.append(f"{pad}만약 {py_expr(stmt.test)}")
230          for child in stmt.body:
231              append_py_stmt(child, depth + 1, lines, limit)
232          if stmt.orelse:
233              lines.append(f"{pad}아니면")
234              for child in stmt.orelse:
235                  append_py_stmt(child, depth + 1, lines, limit)
236          return
237      if isinstance(stmt, ast.For):
238          lines.append(f"{pad}반복 {py_expr(stmt.target)} ← {py_expr(stmt.iter)}")
239          for child in stmt.body:
240              append_py_stmt(child, depth + 1, lines, limit)
241          return
242      if isinstance(stmt, ast.While):
243          lines.append(f"{pad}동안 {py_expr(stmt.test)}")
244          for child in stmt.body:
245              append_py_stmt(child, depth + 1, lines, limit)
246          return
247      if isinstance(stmt, ast.With):
248          heads = ", ".join(py_expr(item.context_expr) for item in stmt.items)
249          lines.append(f"{pad}범위 {heads}")
250          for child in stmt.body:
251              append_py_stmt(child, depth + 1, lines, limit)
252          return
253      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Try):
254          lines.append(f"{pad}시도")
255          for child in stmt.body:
256              append_py_stmt(child, depth + 1, lines, limit)
257          for handler in stmt.handlers:
258              label = py_expr(handler.type) if handler.type else "예외"
259              lines.append(f"{pad}실패 {label}")
260              for child in handler.body:
261                  append_py_stmt(child, depth + 1, lines, limit)
262          return
263      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Raise):
264          lines.append(f"{pad}중단 {py_expr(stmt.exc)}".rstrip())
265          return
266      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Continue):
267          lines.append(f"{pad}다음 반복")
268          return
269      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Break):
270          lines.append(f"{pad}반복 종료")
271          return
272      if isinstance(stmt, ast.Delete):
273          lines.append(f"{pad}삭제 {', '.join(py_expr(target) for target in stmt.targets)}")
274          return
275      if isinstance(stmt, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
276          lines.append(f"{pad}모듈을 불러온다")
277          return
278      try:
279          lines.append(f"{pad}{clip_expr(ast.unparse(stmt))}")
280      except Exception:
281          lines.append(f"{pad}문장을 실행한다")
282  
283  
284  def py_pseudocode(node: ast.AST) -> str:
285      lines: list[str] = []
286      if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
287          lines.append(f"클래스 {node.name}")
288          for stmt in node.body:
289              if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr) and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant):
290                  continue
291              if isinstance(stmt, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
292                  lines.append(f"  메서드 {stmt.name}")
293              elif isinstance(stmt, ast.AnnAssign):
294                  lines.append(f"  필드 {py_expr(stmt.target)}")
295              elif isinstance(stmt, ast.Assign):
296                  targets = ", ".join(py_expr(target) for target in stmt.targets)
297                  lines.append(f"  {targets} ← {py_expr(stmt.value)}")
298              else:
299                  append_py_stmt(stmt, 1, lines, 40)
300          return "\n".join(lines)
301      for stmt in getattr(node, "body", []):
302          append_py_stmt(stmt, 0, lines, 40)
303      if len(lines) >= 40:
304          lines.append("…")
305      return "\n".join(lines) if lines else "수행할 문장이 없다"
306  
307  
308  def js_body_lines(lines: list[str], index: int) -> list[str]:
309      blob = "\n".join(lines[index:])
310      start = blob.find("{")
311      if start < 0:
312          return []
313      depth = 0
314      end = start
315      for pos in range(start, len(blob)):
316          char = blob[pos]
317          if char == "{":
318              depth += 1
319          elif char == "}":
320              depth -= 1
321              if depth == 0:
322                  end = pos
323                  break
324      body = blob[start + 1 : end]
325      return body.splitlines()
326  
327  
328  def js_pseudocode(lines: list[str], index: int) -> str:
329      body = js_body_lines(lines, index)
330      out: list[str] = []
331      depth = 0
332      skipping = False
333      skip_depth = 0
334      for raw in body:
335          if len(out) >= 40:
336              break
337          line = raw.strip()
338          if not line or line.startswith("//") or line.startswith("/*") or line.startswith("*"):
339              if "{" in line or "}" in line:
340                  pass
341              else:
342                  continue
343          if skipping:
344              skip_depth += line.count("{") - line.count("}")
345              if skip_depth <= 0:
346                  skipping = False
347              continue
348          nested = re.match(r"function\s+(\w+)\s*\(", line)
349          if nested:
350              out.append(f"{'  ' * depth}{nested.group(1)} 을 정의한다")
351              skip_depth = line.count("{") - line.count("}")
352              if skip_depth > 0:
353                  skipping = True
354              continue
355          open_count = line.count("{")
356          close_count = line.count("}")
357          code = re.sub(r"\s*\{\s*$", "", line).rstrip(";").strip()
358          if re.match(r"\}?\s*else\s+if\b", code):
359              code = re.sub(r"^\}?\s*", "", code)
360          elif re.match(r"\}?\s*else\b", code):
361              code = "else"
362          pad = "  " * depth
363          if code.startswith("if ("):
364              out.append(f"{pad}만약 {clip_expr(code[3:].strip(' ()'))}")
365          elif code.startswith("} else if (") or code.startswith("else if ("):
366              cond = code.split("(", 1)[1].rsplit(")", 1)[0]
367              out.append(f"{pad}아니면 만약 {clip_expr(cond)}")
368          elif code in ("else", "} else"):
369              out.append(f"{pad}아니면")
370          elif code.startswith("for ") or code.startswith("for("):
371              out.append(f"{pad}반복 {clip_expr(code)}")
372          elif code.startswith("while"):
373              out.append(f"{pad}동안 {clip_expr(code)}")
374          elif code.startswith("return"):
375              value = code[6:].strip()
376              out.append(f"{pad}반환 {clip_expr(value)}" if value else f"{pad}반환")
377          elif re.match(r"(?:var|let|const)\s+", code):
378              out.append(f"{pad}{clip_expr(re.sub(r'^(?:var|let|const)\s+', '', code).replace(' = ', ' ← ', 1))}")
379          elif code.startswith("try"):
380              out.append(f"{pad}시도")
381          elif code.startswith("catch"):
382              out.append(f"{pad}실패")
383          elif code.startswith("throw"):
384              out.append(f"{pad}중단 {clip_expr(code[5:].strip())}")
385          elif code and not re.fullmatch(r"[\s{}\[\]();,]+", code):
386              out.append(f"{pad}{clip_expr(code)}")
387          depth += open_count - close_count
388          if depth < 0:
389              depth = 0
390      if len(out) >= 40:
391          out.append("…")
392      return "\n".join(out) if out else "수행할 문장이 없다"
393  
394  
395  def parse_js(path: Path) -> tuple[str, list[dict]]:
396      lines = read_text(path).splitlines()
397      file_doc = ""
398      if lines and lines[0].strip().startswith("/**"):
399          end = 0
400          while end < len(lines) and "*/" not in lines[end]:
401              end += 1
402          file_doc = jsdoc_before(lines, end + 1)
403      symbols = []
404      for index, line in enumerate(lines):
405          match = re.match(r"\s*function\s+(\w+)\s*\(([^)]*)\)", line)
406          if not match:
407              continue
408          doc = jsdoc_before(lines, index)
409          desc, params, returns = split_jsdoc(doc)
410          if not params and match.group(2).strip():
411              params = [part.strip() for part in match.group(2).split(",") if part.strip()]
412          symbols.append(
413              {
414                  "name": match.group(1),
415                  "signature": f"function {match.group(1)}({match.group(2).strip()})",
416                  "line": index + 1,
417                  "desc": desc,
418                  "params": params,
419                  "returns": returns,
420                  "pseudo": js_pseudocode(lines, index),
421              }
422          )
423      return file_doc, symbols
424  
425  
426  def annotation(node: ast.AST | None) -> str:
427      if node is None:
428          return ""
429      return ast.unparse(node)
430  
431  
432  def py_params(node: ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef) -> list[str]:
433      args = node.args
434      names: list[ast.arg] = list(args.posonlyargs) + list(args.args)
435      default_start = len(names) - len(args.defaults)
436      parts: list[str] = []
437      for index, arg in enumerate(names):
438          if arg.arg in ("self", "cls"):
439              continue
440          label = arg.arg
441          ann = annotation(arg.annotation)
442          if ann:
443              label += f": {ann}"
444          if index >= default_start:
445              default = ast.unparse(args.defaults[index - default_start])
446              label += f" = {default}"
447          parts.append(label)
448      if args.vararg:
449          label = "*" + args.vararg.arg
450          ann = annotation(args.vararg.annotation)
451          parts.append(f"{label}: {ann}" if ann else label)
452      for arg, default in zip(args.kwonlyargs, args.kw_defaults):
453          label = arg.arg
454          ann = annotation(arg.annotation)
455          if ann:
456              label += f": {ann}"
457          if default is not None:
458              label += f" = {ast.unparse(default)}"
459          parts.append(label)
460      if args.kwarg:
461          label = "**" + args.kwarg.arg
462          ann = annotation(args.kwarg.annotation)
463          parts.append(f"{label}: {ann}" if ann else label)
464      return parts
465  
466  
467  def py_signature(node: ast.AST) -> str:
468      if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
469          bases = ", ".join(ast.unparse(base) for base in node.bases)
470          return f"class {node.name}({bases})" if bases else f"class {node.name}"
471      assert isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
472      prefix = "async def" if isinstance(node, ast.AsyncFunctionDef) else "def"
473      ret = annotation(node.returns)
474      tail = f" -> {ret}" if ret else ""
475      return f"{prefix} {node.name}({ast.unparse(node.args)}){tail}"
476  
477  
478  def iter_py_symbols(node: ast.AST, prefix: str = ""):
479      for child in getattr(node, "body", []):
480          if isinstance(child, ast.ClassDef):
481              name = f"{prefix}{child.name}"
482              yield name, child
483              yield from iter_py_symbols(child, name + ".")
484          elif isinstance(child, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
485              name = f"{prefix}{child.name}"
486              yield name, child
487              yield from iter_py_symbols(child, name + ".")
488  
489  
490  def parse_py(path: Path) -> tuple[str, list[dict]]:
491      tree = ast.parse(read_text(path))
492      file_doc = ast.get_docstring(tree) or ""
493      symbols = []
494      for name, node in iter_py_symbols(tree):
495          desc = ast.get_docstring(node) or ""
496          params: list[str] = []
497          returns = ""
498          if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
499              params = py_params(node)
500              returns = annotation(node.returns)
501          symbols.append(
502              {
503                  "name": name,
504                  "signature": py_signature(node),
505                  "line": node.lineno,
506                  "desc": " ".join(desc.split()),
507                  "params": params,
508                  "returns": returns,
509                  "pseudo": py_pseudocode(node),
510              }
511          )
512      return file_doc, symbols
513  
514  
515  CODE_SUFFIXES = {
516      ".vb",
517      ".html",
518      ".htm",
519      ".js",
520      ".css",
521      ".py",
522      ".cs",
523      ".java",
524      ".c",
525      ".cpp",
526      ".h",
527      ".config",
528      ".vbproj",
529      ".sln",
530      ".resx",
531      ".settings",
532      ".suo",
533      ".user",
534  }
535  
536  
537  def file_comment(path: Path) -> str:
538      text = read_text(path)
539      if path.suffix.lower() == ".html":
540          match = re.match(r"<!--\s*(.*?)\s*-->", text, re.S)
541          return " ".join(match.group(1).split()) if match else ""
542      if path.suffix.lower() == ".css":
543          if text.startswith("@charset"):
544              text = text.split("\n", 1)[1]
545          match = re.search(r"/\*\s*(.*?)\s*\*/", text, re.S)
546          return " ".join(match.group(1).split()) if match else ""
547      return ""
548  
549  
550  def add_symbol(doc: Doc, path: Path, symbol: dict) -> None:
551      doc.h4(symbol["name"])
552      doc.p(f"`{rel(path)}:{symbol['line']}`")
553      doc.code(symbol["signature"])
554      doc.p(symbol["desc"] or "설명이 없습니다.")
555      params = symbol["params"] or ["없음"]
556      doc.ul([f"인자: {', '.join(params)}", f"반환: {symbol['returns'] or '없음'}"])
557      pseudo = symbol.get("pseudo") or ""
558      if pseudo:
559          doc.p("유사코드")
560          doc.code(pseudo)
561  
562  
563  def add_code_file(doc: Doc, path: Path, file_doc: str, symbols: list[dict]) -> None:
564      doc.h3(rel(path))
565      if file_doc:
566          doc.p(file_doc)
567      for symbol in symbols:
568          add_symbol(doc, path, symbol)
569  
570  
571  def data_role(path: Path) -> str:
572      name = path.name
573      suffix = path.suffix.lower()
574      if suffix in {".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".gif", ".webp", ".bmp"}:
575          return "학습·과제 화면 캡처 또는 도표"
576      if "과제분류하기" in path.parts:
577          return "과제 제출 원본"
578      if "독서" in name:
579          return "독서 노트"
580      if "수강" in name or "영상" in name:
581          return "수강 노트"
582      if suffix == ".hwpx":
583          return "한글 문서 원본. 요약 빌드에서는 제외한다"
584      return "학습 기록 원본"
585  
586  
587  def is_generated_data(path: Path) -> bool:
588      parts = set(path.parts)
589      if parts & {"obj", "bin", "My Project"}:
590          return True
591      if path.name.endswith(".Designer.vb"):
592          return True
593      if path.suffix.lower() in {".exe", ".dll", ".pdb", ".cache", ".suo", ".user"}:
594          return True
595      return False
596  
597  
598  def collect_originals() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
599      data = ROOT / "study_note_record_organization" / "data"
600      grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
601      if not data.is_dir():
602          return grouped
603      for path in sorted(data.rglob("*")):
604          if not path.is_file() or is_generated_data(path):
605              continue
606          if ".vs" in path.parts or path.suffix.lower() in CODE_SUFFIXES:
607              continue
608          label = mask_privacy(f"{rel(path)} — {data_role(path)}")
609          grouped.setdefault(path.suffix.lower() or "(확장자 없음)", []).append(label)
610      return grouped
611

765  
766  def find_browser() -> Path | None:
767      local = os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA", "")
768      candidates = [
769          Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
770          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
771          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"),
772          Path(local) / "Google" / "Chrome" / "Application" / "chrome.exe",
773      ]
774      for candidate in candidates:
775          if candidate.is_file():
776              return candidate
777      return None
778  
779  
780  def write_pdf(html_path: Path, pdf_path: Path) -> None:
781      browser = find_browser()
782      if browser is None:
783          raise SystemExit("PDF로 인쇄할 Edge 또는 Chrome을 찾지 못했습니다.")
784      if pdf_path.exists():
785          pdf_path.unlink()
786      profile = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="code-ref-pdf-"))
787      url = html_path.resolve().as_uri()
788      command = [
789          str(browser),
790          "--headless=new",
791          "--disable-gpu",
792          "--no-first-run",
793          "--no-pdf-header-footer",
794          f"--user-data-dir={profile}",
795          f"--print-to-pdf={pdf_path.resolve()}",
796          url,
797      ]
798      try:
799          subprocess.run(command, check=True, timeout=120)
800      finally:
801          shutil.rmtree(profile, ignore_errors=True)
802      if not pdf_path.is_file() or pdf_path.stat().st_size < 1000:
803          raise SystemExit(f"PDF가 만들어지지 않았습니다: {pdf_path}")
804  
805  
806  def main() -> None:
807      doc = build()
808      MD_PATH.write_text(doc.render_md(), encoding="utf-8")
809      HTML_PATH.write_text(doc.render_html(), encoding="utf-8")
810      write_pdf(HTML_PATH, PDF_PATH)
811      print(f"Wrote {MD_PATH}")
812      print(f"Wrote {HTML_PATH}")
813      print(f"Wrote {PDF_PATH} ({PDF_PATH.stat().st_size // 1024} KB)")
814  
815  
816  if __name__ == "__main__":
817      main()

docs/build_codebook.py

  1  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
  2  """코드북 마크다운을 이어 한글 PDF로 만든다."""
  3  from __future__ import annotations
  4  
  5  import html
  6  import os
  7  import re
  8  import shutil
  9  import subprocess
 10  import tempfile
 11  from pathlib import Path
 12  
 13  ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
 14  CHAPTERS = ROOT / "codebook"
 15  MD_PATH = ROOT / "codebook.md"
 16  HTML_PATH = ROOT / "codebook.html"
 17  PDF_PATH = ROOT / "codebook.pdf"
 18  
 19  INLINE_CODE = re.compile(r"`([^`]+)`")
 20  BOLD = re.compile(r"\*\*(.+?)\*\*")
 21  
 22  
 23  def inline(text: str) -> str:
 24      escaped = html.escape(text)
 25      escaped = INLINE_CODE.sub(lambda match: f"<code>{match.group(1)}</code>", escaped)
 26      escaped = BOLD.sub(lambda match: f"<strong>{match.group(1)}</strong>", escaped)
 27      return escaped
 28  
 29  
 30  def markdown_to_html(text: str) -> str:
 31      lines = text.splitlines()
 32      parts: list[str] = []
 33      index = 0
 34      while index < len(lines):
 35          line = lines[index]
 36          if line.startswith("```"):
 37              body: list[str] = []
 38              index += 1
 39              while index < len(lines) and not lines[index].startswith("```"):
 40                  body.append(lines[index])
 41                  index += 1
 42              index += 1
 43              parts.append(f'<pre class="code">{html.escape(chr(10).join(body))}</pre>')
 44              continue
 45          if not line.strip():
 46              index += 1
 47              continue
 48          if line.startswith("#"):
 49              level = len(line) - len(line.lstrip("#"))
 50              level = min(max(level, 1), 4)
 51              title = line[level:].strip()
 52              parts.append(f"<h{level}>{inline(title)}</h{level}>")
 53              index += 1
 54              continue
 55          if line.startswith("- "):
 56              items: list[str] = []
 57              while index < len(lines) and lines[index].startswith("- "):
 58                  items.append(f"<li>{inline(lines[index][2:].strip())}</li>")
 59                  index += 1
 60              parts.append("<ul>" + "".join(items) + "</ul>")
 61              continue
 62          if re.match(r"\d+\.\s+", line):
 63              items = []
 64              while index < len(lines) and re.match(r"\d+\.\s+", lines[index]):
 65                  item_text = re.sub(r"^\d+\.\s+", "", lines[index]).strip()
 66                  items.append(f"<li>{inline(item_text)}</li>")
 67                  index += 1
 68              parts.append("<ol>" + "".join(items) + "</ol>")
 69              continue
 70          paragraph = [line.strip()]
 71          index += 1
 72          while (
 73              index < len(lines)
 74              and lines[index].strip()
 75              and not lines[index].startswith(("#", "- ", "```"))
 76              and not re.match(r"\d+\.\s+", lines[index])
 77          ):
 78              paragraph.append(lines[index].strip())
 79              index += 1
 80          parts.append(f"<p>{inline(' '.join(paragraph))}</p>")
 81      return "\n".join(parts)
 82  
 83  
 84  def combined_markdown() -> str:
 85      files = sorted(CHAPTERS.glob("*.md"))
 86      if not files:
 87          raise SystemExit(f"코드북 마크다운이 없습니다: {CHAPTERS}")
 88      chunks = [path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() for path in files]
 89      return "\n\n".join(chunks) + "\n"
 90  
 91  
 92  HTML_CSS = """
 93  @page { margin: 18mm 16mm; }
 94  body { font-family: "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif; font-size: 11pt; line-height: 1.55; color: #1c1c1c; }
 95  h1 { font-size: 20pt; margin: 0 0 0.6em; page-break-before: always; }
 96  h1:first-of-type { page-break-before: auto; }
 97  h2 { font-size: 15pt; margin: 1.2em 0 0.4em; page-break-after: avoid; }
 98  h3 { font-size: 12.5pt; margin: 1em 0 0.3em; page-break-after: avoid; }
 99  p, li { orphans: 3; widows: 3; }
100  ul, ol { margin: 0.2em 0 0.8em; padding-left: 1.2em; }
101  code { font-family: Consolas, "Malgun Gothic", monospace; font-size: 9.5pt; }
102  pre { font-family: Consolas, "Malgun Gothic", monospace; font-size: 9pt; background: #f3f3f3;
103        padding: 8px 10px; white-space: pre-wrap; }
104  """
105  
106  
107  def render_html(body: str) -> str:
108      return "\n".join(
109          [
110              "<!DOCTYPE html>",
111              '<html lang="ko">',
112              "<head>",
113              '<meta charset="utf-8" />',
114              "<title>코드북</title>",
115              "<style>",
116              HTML_CSS,
117              "</style>",
118              "</head>",
119              "<body>",
120              body,
121              "</body>",
122              "</html>",
123          ]
124      )
125  
126  
127  def find_browser() -> Path | None:
128      local = os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA", "")
129      candidates = [
130          Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
131          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
132          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"),
133          Path(local) / "Google" / "Chrome" / "Application" / "chrome.exe",
134      ]
135      for candidate in candidates:
136          if candidate.is_file():
137              return candidate
138      return None

141  def write_pdf(html_path: Path, pdf_path: Path) -> None:
142      browser = find_browser()
143      if browser is None:
144          raise SystemExit("PDF로 인쇄할 Edge 또는 Chrome을 찾지 못했습니다.")
145      if pdf_path.exists():
146          pdf_path.unlink()
147      profile = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="codebook-pdf-"))
148      command = [
149          str(browser),
150          "--headless=new",
151          "--disable-gpu",
152          "--no-first-run",
153          "--no-pdf-header-footer",
154          f"--user-data-dir={profile}",
155          f"--print-to-pdf={pdf_path.resolve()}",
156          html_path.resolve().as_uri(),
157      ]
158      try:
159          subprocess.run(command, check=True, timeout=120)
160      finally:
161          shutil.rmtree(profile, ignore_errors=True)
162      if not pdf_path.is_file() or pdf_path.stat().st_size < 1000:
163          raise SystemExit(f"PDF가 만들어지지 않았습니다: {pdf_path}")
164  
165  
166  def main() -> None:
167      markdown = combined_markdown()
168      MD_PATH.write_text(markdown, encoding="utf-8")
169      HTML_PATH.write_text(render_html(markdown_to_html(markdown)), encoding="utf-8")
170      write_pdf(HTML_PATH, PDF_PATH)
171      print(f"Wrote {MD_PATH}")
172      print(f"Wrote {HTML_PATH}")
173      print(f"Wrote {PDF_PATH} ({PDF_PATH.stat().st_size // 1024} KB)")
174  
175  
176  if __name__ == "__main__":
177      main()

docs/build_portfolio_book.py

  1  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
  2  """포트폴리오 북 마크다운 챕터들을 통합하여 A4 규격의 한글 PDF 단행본으로 빌드한다.
  3  
  4  마크다운 표, 인용구, 코드 블록, 인라인 링크 및 Mermaid 다이어그램을 파싱하여
  5  Microsoft Edge 또는 Google Chrome 헤드리스 엔진으로 고품질 인쇄용 PDF를 생성한다.
  6  """
  7  from __future__ import annotations
  8  
  9  import hashlib
 10  import html
 11  import os
 12  import re
 13  import shutil
 14  import subprocess
 15  import tempfile
 16  import time
 17  from pathlib import Path
 18  
 19  from PIL import Image, ImageChops
 20  
 21  ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
 22  CHAPTERS = ROOT / "portfolio-book"
 23  DIAGRAMS = CHAPTERS / "diagrams"
 24  MD_PATH = ROOT / "portfolio-book.md"
 25  HTML_PATH = ROOT / "portfolio-book.html"
 26  PDF_PATH = ROOT / "portfolio-book.pdf"
 27  USED_DIAGRAMS: set[str] = set()
 28  
 29  INLINE_CODE = re.compile(r"`([^`]+)`")
 30  BOLD = re.compile(r"\*\*(.+?)\*\*")
 31  LINK = re.compile(r"\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)]+)\)")
 32  
 33  
 34  def inline(text: str) -> str:
 35      escaped = html.escape(text)
 36      escaped = INLINE_CODE.sub(lambda m: f"<code>{m.group(1)}</code>", escaped)
 37      escaped = BOLD.sub(lambda m: f"<strong>{m.group(1)}</strong>", escaped)
 38      escaped = LINK.sub(lambda m: f'<a href="{m.group(2)}">{m.group(1)}</a>', escaped)
 39      return escaped
 40  
 41  
 42  def find_browser() -> Path | None:
 43      local = os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA", "")
 44      candidates = [
 45          Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
 46          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
 47          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"),
 48          Path(local) / "Google" / "Chrome" / "Application" / "chrome.exe",
 49      ]
 50      for candidate in candidates:
 51          if candidate.is_file():
 52              return candidate
 53      return None
 54  
 55  
 56  def diagram_page(source: str) -> str:
 57      escaped = html.escape(source)
 58      return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
 59  <html lang="ko">
 60  <head>
 61  <meta charset="utf-8" />
 62  <style>
 63    html, body {{ margin: 0; background: #fff; }}
 64    body {{ padding: 24px; }}
 65    .mermaid {{ font-family: "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif; }}
 66  </style>
 67  </head>
 68  <body>
 69  <pre class="mermaid">{escaped}</pre>
 70  <script type="module">
 71    import mermaid from "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/mermaid@11/dist/mermaid.esm.min.mjs";
 72    mermaid.initialize({{ startOnLoad: true, theme: "neutral", fontFamily: "Malgun Gothic, sans-serif" }});
 73  </script>
 74  </body>
 75  </html>
 76  """
 77  
 78  
 79  def crop_whitespace(path: Path) -> None:
 80      image = Image.open(path).convert("RGB")
 81      background = Image.new("RGB", image.size, (255, 255, 255))
 82      box = ImageChops.difference(image, background).getbbox()
 83      if box is None:
 84          raise SystemExit(f"차트가 비어 있습니다: {path.name}")
 85      pad = 16
 86      left = max(box[0] - pad, 0)
 87      top = max(box[1] - pad, 0)
 88      right = min(box[2] + pad, image.width)
 89      bottom = min(box[3] + pad, image.height)
 90      cropped = image.crop((left, top, right, bottom))
 91      if cropped.width < 40 or cropped.height < 40:
 92          raise SystemExit(f"차트가 너무 작습니다: {path.name}")
 93      cropped.save(path)
 94  
 95  
 96  def render_diagram(source: str) -> str:
 97      digest = hashlib.sha256(source.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]
 98      USED_DIAGRAMS.add(digest)
 99      DIAGRAMS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
100      png = DIAGRAMS / f"{digest}.png"
101      if not png.is_file():
102          browser = find_browser()
103          if browser is None:
104              escaped = html.escape(source.strip())
105              return f'<pre class="code diagram-fallback">{escaped}</pre>'
106          html_path = DIAGRAMS / f"{digest}.html"
107          html_path.write_text(diagram_page(source), encoding="utf-8")
108          profile = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="book-chart-"))
109          command = [
110              str(browser),
111              "--headless=new",
112              "--disable-gpu",
113              "--no-first-run",
114              "--hide-scrollbars",
115              "--force-device-scale-factor=2",
116              "--virtual-time-budget=12000",
117              "--window-size=1400,2200",
118              f"--user-data-dir={profile}",
119              f"--screenshot={png.resolve()}",
120              html_path.resolve().as_uri(),
121          ]
122          try:
123              subprocess.run(command, check=True, timeout=60)
124              deadline = time.time() + 8
125              while not png.is_file() and time.time() < deadline:
126                  time.sleep(0.3)
127              crop_whitespace(png)
128          except Exception as e:
129              print(f"[경고] 차트 렌더링 실패 ({digest}): {e}")
130              escaped = html.escape(source.strip())
131              return f'<pre class="code diagram-fallback">{escaped}</pre>'
132          finally:
133              shutil.rmtree(profile, ignore_errors=True)
134              if html_path.exists():
135                  html_path.unlink()
136      rel = png.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
137      return f'<figure class="chart"><img src="{rel}" alt="다이어그램" /></figure>'
138  
139  
140  def parse_table(lines: list[str]) -> str:
141      rows: list[list[str]] = []
142      for line in lines:
143          raw_cells = [c.strip() for c in line.strip().strip("|").split("|")]
144          rows.append(raw_cells)
145      if not rows:
146          return ""
147      header = rows[0]
148      data_rows = rows[1:]
149      # Check if the next row is a separator line (e.g. :---: | :---)
150      if data_rows and all(re.match(r"^:?-+:?$", cell) for cell in data_rows[0]):
151          data_rows = data_rows[1:]
152  
153      html_parts = ['<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr>']
154      for cell in header:
155          html_parts.append(f"<th>{inline(cell)}</th>")
156      html_parts.append("</tr></thead><tbody>")
157      for r in data_rows:
158          html_parts.append("<tr>")
159          for cell in r:
160              html_parts.append(f"<td>{inline(cell)}</td>")
161          html_parts.append("</tr>")
162      html_parts.append("</tbody></table></div>")
163      return "".join(html_parts)
164  
165  
166  def markdown_to_html(text: str) -> str:
167      lines = text.splitlines()
168      parts: list[str] = []
169      index = 0
170      first_h1 = True
171  
172      while index < len(lines):
173          line = lines[index]
174  
175          # Fenced code blocks
176          if line.startswith("```"):
177              language = line[3:].strip()
178              body: list[str] = []
179              index += 1
180              while index < len(lines) and not lines[index].startswith("```"):
181                  body.append(lines[index])
182                  index += 1
183              index += 1
184              source = "\n".join(body).strip() + "\n"
185              if language == "mermaid":
186                  parts.append(render_diagram(source))
187              else:
188                  parts.append(f'<pre class="code"><code>{html.escape(source)}</code></pre>')
189              continue
190  
191          stripped = line.strip()
192          if not stripped:
193              index += 1
194              continue
195  
196          # Horizontal Rule
197          if stripped in ("---", "***", "___"):
198              parts.append('<hr class="divider" />')
199              index += 1
200              continue
201  
202          # Headings
203          if line.startswith("#"):
204              level = len(line) - len(line.lstrip("#"))
205              level = min(max(level, 1), 4)
206              title = line[level:].strip()
207              cls = ""
208              if level == 1:
209                  if first_h1:
210                      cls = ' class="book-title"'
211                      first_h1 = False
212                  else:
213                      cls = ' class="chapter-title"'
214              parts.append(f"<h{level}{cls}>{inline(title)}</h{level}>")
215              index += 1
216              continue
217  
218          # Blockquote
219          if line.startswith(">"):
220              quote_lines: list[str] = []
221              while index < len(lines) and (lines[index].startswith(">") or (lines[index].strip() and not lines[index].startswith("#"))):
222                  curr = lines[index]
223                  if curr.startswith(">"):
224                      curr = curr[1:].strip()
225                  else:
226                      curr = curr.strip()
227                  quote_lines.append(curr)
228                  index += 1
229              quote_body = "<br />".join(inline(ql) for ql in quote_lines if ql)
230              parts.append(f"<blockquote><p>{quote_body}</p></blockquote>")
231              continue
232  
233          # Tables (lines starting and ending with |)
234          if stripped.startswith("|") and stripped.endswith("|"):
235              table_lines: list[str] = []
236              while index < len(lines) and lines[index].strip().startswith("|") and lines[index].strip().endswith("|"):
237                  table_lines.append(lines[index])
238                  index += 1
239              parts.append(parse_table(table_lines))
240              continue
241  
242          # Unordered Lists
243          if line.startswith("- ") or line.startswith("* "):
244              items: list[str] = []
245              while index < len(lines) and (lines[index].startswith("- ") or lines[index].startswith("* ")):
246                  item_content = lines[index][2:].strip()
247                  items.append(f"<li>{inline(item_content)}</li>")
248                  index += 1
249              parts.append(f"<ul>{''.join(items)}</ul>")
250              continue
251  
252          # Ordered Lists
253          ordered = re.match(r"^\d+\.\s+", line)
254          if ordered:
255              items = []
256              while index < len(lines) and re.match(r"^\d+\.\s+", lines[index]):
257                  item_text = re.sub(r"^\d+\.\s+", "", lines[index]).strip()
258                  items.append(f"<li>{inline(item_text)}</li>")
259                  index += 1
260              parts.append(f"<ol>{''.join(items)}</ol>")
261              continue
262  
263          # Paragraphs
264          paragraph = [line.strip()]
265          index += 1
266          while (
267              index < len(lines)
268              and lines[index].strip()
269              and not lines[index].startswith(("#", "- ", "* ", ">", "```"))
270              and not (lines[index].strip().startswith("|") and lines[index].strip().endswith("|"))
271              and not lines[index].strip() in ("---", "***", "___")
272              and not re.match(r"^\d+\.\s+", lines[index])
273          ):
274              paragraph.append(lines[index].strip())
275              index += 1
276          parts.append(f"<p>{inline(' '.join(paragraph))}</p>")
277  
278      return "\n".join(parts)
279  
280  
281  def combined_markdown() -> str:
282      files = sorted(CHAPTERS.glob("*.md"))
283      if not files:
284          raise SystemExit(f"포트폴리오 북 챕터 마크다운이 없습니다: {CHAPTERS}")
285      chunks = [path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() for path in files]
286      return "\n\n".join(chunks) + "\n"

497  def render_html(body: str) -> str:
498      return "\n".join(
499          [
500              "<!DOCTYPE html>",
501              '<html lang="ko">',
502              "<head>",
503              '<meta charset="utf-8" />',
504              "<title>코탐록 포트폴리오 북 (Kotamrok Portfolio Book)</title>",
505              "<style>",
506              HTML_CSS,
507              "</style>",
508              "</head>",
509              "<body>",
510              body,
511              "</body>",
512              "</html>",
513          ]
514      )
515  
516  
517  def write_pdf(html_path: Path, pdf_path: Path) -> None:
518      browser = find_browser()
519      if browser is None:
520          raise SystemExit("PDF로 인쇄할 Edge 또는 Chrome을 찾지 못했습니다.")
521      if pdf_path.exists():
522          pdf_path.unlink()
523      profile = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="portfolio-book-pdf-"))
524      command = [
525          str(browser),
526          "--headless=new",
527          "--disable-gpu",
528          "--no-first-run",
529          "--no-pdf-header-footer",
530          f"--user-data-dir={profile}",
531          f"--print-to-pdf={pdf_path.resolve()}",
532          html_path.resolve().as_uri(),
533      ]
534      try:
535          subprocess.run(command, check=True, timeout=120)
536      finally:
537          shutil.rmtree(profile, ignore_errors=True)
538      if not pdf_path.is_file() or pdf_path.stat().st_size < 1000:
539          raise SystemExit(f"PDF가 만들어지지 않았습니다: {pdf_path}")
540  
541  
542  def main() -> None:
543      print("[1/4] 포트폴리오 북 마크다운 챕터 병합 중...")
544      markdown = combined_markdown()
545      MD_PATH.write_text(markdown, encoding="utf-8")
546      print(f"  -> 통합 마크다운 저장: {MD_PATH}")
547  
548      print("[2/4] 마크다운 -> HTML 변환 및 다이어그램 렌더링 중...")
549      html_content = render_html(markdown_to_html(markdown))
550      HTML_PATH.write_text(html_content, encoding="utf-8")
551      print(f"  -> HTML 저장: {HTML_PATH}")
552  
553      print("[3/4] 헤드리스 브라우저 A4 PDF 변환 중...")
554      write_pdf(HTML_PATH, PDF_PATH)
555      print(f"  -> PDF 완성: {PDF_PATH} ({PDF_PATH.stat().st_size // 1024} KB)")
556  
557      print("[4/4] 빌드 완료! 성공적으로 포트폴리오 북이 생성되었습니다.")
558  
559  
560  if __name__ == "__main__":
561      main()

docs/build_source_bundle.py

  1  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
  2  """주석이 붙은 소스를 파일 단위로 이어 하나의 PDF로 만든다."""
  3  from __future__ import annotations
  4  
  5  import html
  6  import os
  7  import shutil
  8  import subprocess
  9  import tempfile
 10  from pathlib import Path
 11  
 12  ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
 13  DOCS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
 14  HTML_PATH = DOCS / "source-bundle.html"
 15  PDF_PATH = DOCS / "source-bundle.pdf"
 16  
 17  SUFFIXES = {".html", ".css", ".js", ".py"}
 18  SKIP_DIRS = {
 19      "data",
 20      "output",
 21      "sources",
 22      "__pycache__",
 23      ".vs",
 24      "obj",
 25      "bin",
 26      "node_modules",
 27  }
 28  SKIP_FILES = {"data.js", "hero-images.js"}
 29  
 30  
 31  def include(path: Path) -> bool:
 32      if path.suffix.lower() not in SUFFIXES:
 33          return False
 34      if path.name in SKIP_FILES or path.name.endswith(".min.js"):
 35          return False
 36      if any(part in SKIP_DIRS for part in path.parts):
 37          return False
 38      if DOCS in path.parents and path.suffix.lower() == ".html":
 39          return False
 40      return True
 41  
 42  
 43  def source_files() -> list[Path]:
 44      files = [path for path in ROOT.rglob("*") if path.is_file() and include(path)]
 45      return sorted(files, key=lambda path: path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix())
 46  
 47  
 48  def file_block(path: Path) -> str:
 49      rel = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
 50      text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 51      lines = text.splitlines()
 52      width = max(len(str(len(lines))), 2)
 53      numbered = "\n".join(f"{index:>{width}}  {line}" for index, line in enumerate(lines, start=1))
 54      return f"<h2>{html.escape(rel)}</h2>\n<pre>{html.escape(numbered)}</pre>"
 55  
 56  
 57  def render_html(blocks: list[str]) -> str:
 58      intro = (
 59          "<h1>소스 모음</h1>"
 60          "<p>화면과 빌드 소스를 파일 순서대로 이어 두었습니다. "
 61          "주석은 코드 옆에 그대로 있습니다. "
 62          "data/ 과제 코드, 압축된 라이브러리, 빌드가 만든 data.js, hero-images.js, sources JSON은 넣지 않았습니다.</p>"
 63      )
 64      css = """
 65  @page { margin: 12mm 10mm; }
 66  body { font-family: "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif; color: #1c1c1c; }
 67  h1 { font-size: 18pt; margin: 0 0 0.4em; }
 68  h2 { font-size: 12pt; margin: 1.2em 0 0.3em; page-break-before: always; page-break-after: avoid; }
 69  h2:first-of-type { page-break-before: auto; }
 70  p { font-size: 10.5pt; line-height: 1.5; }
 71  pre { font-family: Consolas, "Malgun Gothic", monospace; font-size: 8pt; line-height: 1.35;
 72        white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-all; margin: 0; }
 73  """
 74      return "\n".join(
 75          [
 76              "<!DOCTYPE html>",
 77              '<html lang="ko">',
 78              "<head>",
 79              '<meta charset="utf-8" />',
 80              "<title>소스 모음</title>",
 81              "<style>",
 82              css,
 83              "</style>",
 84              "</head>",
 85              "<body>",
 86              intro,
 87              "\n".join(blocks),
 88              "</body>",
 89              "</html>",
 90          ]
 91      )
 92  
 93  
 94  def find_browser() -> Path | None:
 95      local = os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA", "")
 96      candidates = [
 97          Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
 98          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
 99          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"),
100          Path(local) / "Google" / "Chrome" / "Application" / "chrome.exe",
101      ]
102      for candidate in candidates:
103          if candidate.is_file():
104              return candidate
105      return None
106  
107  
108  def write_pdf(html_path: Path, pdf_path: Path) -> None:
109      browser = find_browser()
110      if browser is None:
111          raise SystemExit("PDF로 인쇄할 Edge 또는 Chrome을 찾지 못했습니다.")
112      if pdf_path.exists():
113          pdf_path.unlink()
114      profile = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="source-bundle-"))
115      command = [
116          str(browser),
117          "--headless=new",
118          "--disable-gpu",
119          "--no-first-run",
120          "--no-pdf-header-footer",
121          f"--user-data-dir={profile}",
122          f"--print-to-pdf={pdf_path.resolve()}",
123          html_path.resolve().as_uri(),
124      ]
125      try:
126          subprocess.run(command, check=True, timeout=180)
127      finally:
128          shutil.rmtree(profile, ignore_errors=True)
129      if not pdf_path.is_file() or pdf_path.stat().st_size < 1000:
130          raise SystemExit(f"PDF가 만들어지지 않았습니다: {pdf_path}")
131  
132  
133  def main() -> None:
134      files = source_files()
135      if not files:
136          raise SystemExit("묶을 소스 파일이 없습니다.")
137      HTML_PATH.write_text(render_html([file_block(path) for path in files]), encoding="utf-8")
138      write_pdf(HTML_PATH, PDF_PATH)
139      print(f"Files {len(files)}")
140      print(f"Wrote {PDF_PATH} ({PDF_PATH.stat().st_size // 1024} KB)")
141  
142  
143  if __name__ == "__main__":
144      main()

docs/build_specs.py

  1  # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
  2  """기술명세 마크다운을 이어 한글 PDF로 만든다.
  3  
  4  Mermaid 차트는 Edge로 그린 뒤 잘라서 PNG로 넣는다.
  5  """
  6  from __future__ import annotations
  7  
  8  import hashlib
  9  import html
 10  import os
 11  import re
 12  import shutil
 13  import subprocess
 14  import tempfile
 15  import time
 16  from pathlib import Path
 17  
 18  from PIL import Image, ImageChops
 19  
 20  ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
 21  SPECS = ROOT / "specs"
 22  DIAGRAMS = SPECS / "diagrams"
 23  MD_PATH = ROOT / "technical-specs.md"
 24  HTML_PATH = ROOT / "technical-specs.html"
 25  PDF_PATH = ROOT / "technical-specs.pdf"
 26  USED_DIAGRAMS: set[str] = set()
 27  
 28  INLINE_CODE = re.compile(r"`([^`]+)`")
 29  BOLD = re.compile(r"\*\*(.+?)\*\*")
 30  
 31  
 32  def inline(text: str) -> str:
 33      escaped = html.escape(text)
 34      escaped = INLINE_CODE.sub(lambda match: f"<code>{match.group(1)}</code>", escaped)
 35      escaped = BOLD.sub(lambda match: f"<strong>{match.group(1)}</strong>", escaped)
 36      return escaped
 37  
 38  
 39  def markdown_to_html(text: str) -> str:
 40      lines = text.splitlines()
 41      parts: list[str] = []
 42      index = 0
 43      while index < len(lines):
 44          line = lines[index]
 45          if line.startswith("```"):
 46              language = line[3:].strip()
 47              body: list[str] = []
 48              index += 1
 49              while index < len(lines) and not lines[index].startswith("```"):
 50                  body.append(lines[index])
 51                  index += 1
 52              index += 1
 53              source = "\n".join(body).strip() + "\n"
 54              if language == "mermaid":
 55                  parts.append(render_diagram(source))
 56              else:
 57                  parts.append(f'<pre class="code">{html.escape(source)}</pre>')
 58              continue
 59          if not line.strip():
 60              index += 1
 61              continue
 62          if line.startswith("#"):
 63              level = len(line) - len(line.lstrip("#"))
 64              level = min(max(level, 1), 4)
 65              title = line[level:].strip()
 66              parts.append(f"<h{level}>{inline(title)}</h{level}>")
 67              index += 1
 68              continue
 69          if line.startswith("- "):
 70              items: list[str] = []
 71              while index < len(lines) and lines[index].startswith("- "):
 72                  items.append(f"<li>{inline(lines[index][2:].strip())}</li>")
 73                  index += 1
 74              parts.append("<ul>" + "".join(items) + "</ul>")
 75              continue
 76          ordered = re.match(r"\d+\.\s+", line)
 77          if ordered:
 78              items = []
 79              while index < len(lines) and re.match(r"\d+\.\s+", lines[index]):
 80                  item_text = re.sub(r"^\d+\.\s+", "", lines[index]).strip()
 81                  items.append(f"<li>{inline(item_text)}</li>")
 82                  index += 1
 83              parts.append("<ol>" + "".join(items) + "</ol>")
 84              continue
 85          paragraph = [line.strip()]
 86          index += 1
 87          while (
 88              index < len(lines)
 89              and lines[index].strip()
 90              and not lines[index].startswith(("#", "- ", "```"))
 91              and not re.match(r"\d+\.\s+", lines[index])
 92          ):
 93              paragraph.append(lines[index].strip())
 94              index += 1
 95          parts.append(f"<p>{inline(' '.join(paragraph))}</p>")
 96      return "\n".join(parts)
 97  
 98  
 99  def combined_markdown() -> str:
100      files = sorted(SPECS.glob("*.md"))
101      if not files:
102          raise SystemExit(f"명세 마크다운이 없습니다: {SPECS}")
103      chunks = [path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() for path in files]
104      return "\n\n".join(chunks) + "\n"

107  HTML_CSS = """
108  @page { margin: 18mm 16mm; }
109  body { font-family: "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif; font-size: 11pt; line-height: 1.55; color: #1c1c1c; }
110  h1 { font-size: 20pt; margin: 0 0 0.6em; page-break-before: always; }
111  h1:first-of-type { page-break-before: auto; }
112  h2 { font-size: 15pt; margin: 1.2em 0 0.4em; page-break-after: avoid; }
113  h3 { font-size: 12.5pt; margin: 1em 0 0.3em; page-break-after: avoid; }
114  p, li { orphans: 3; widows: 3; }
115  ul { margin: 0.2em 0 0.8em; padding-left: 1.2em; }
116  code { font-family: Consolas, "Malgun Gothic", monospace; font-size: 9.5pt; }
117  pre { font-family: Consolas, "Malgun Gothic", monospace; font-size: 9pt; background: #f3f3f3;
118        padding: 8px 10px; white-space: pre-wrap; }
119  figure.chart { margin: 0.4em 0 1em; text-align: center; page-break-inside: avoid; break-inside: avoid; }
120  figure.chart img { max-width: 100%; max-height: 230mm; width: auto; height: auto; }
121  """

126          [
127              "<!DOCTYPE html>",
128              '<html lang="ko">',
129              "<head>",
130              '<meta charset="utf-8" />',
131              "<title>기술명세</title>",
132              "<style>",
133              HTML_CSS,
134              "</style>",
135              "</head>",
136              "<body>",
137              body,
138              "</body>",
139              "</html>",
140          ]
141      )

144  def find_browser() -> Path | None:
145      local = os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA", "")
146      candidates = [
147          Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
148          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"),
149          Path(r"C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"),
150          Path(local) / "Google" / "Chrome" / "Application" / "chrome.exe",
151      ]
152      for candidate in candidates:
153          if candidate.is_file():
154              return candidate
155      return None

157  
158  ACTOR_LINE = re.compile(r"^(\w+)\[(.+)\]$")
159  CASE_LINE = re.compile(r"^(\w+)\(\[(.+)\]\)$")
160  BOUNDARY_LINE = re.compile(r"^subgraph\s+\w+\[(.+)\]$")
161  ASSOC_LINE = re.compile(r"^(\w+)\s+---\s+(\w+)$")
162  EXTEND_LINE = re.compile(r"^(\w+)\s+-\.->\|([^|]+)\|\s+(\w+)$")
163  BREAK_LINE = re.compile(r"<br\s*/?>")
164  
165  
166  def parse_usecase(source: str) -> tuple[str, list, list, list, list]:
167      actors: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
168      cases: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
169      assocs: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
170      extends: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []
171      boundary = "시스템"
172      for raw in source.splitlines():
173          line = raw.strip()
174          if not line or line.startswith(("%%", "flowchart", "direction")) or line == "end":
175              continue
176          found = BOUNDARY_LINE.match(line)
177          if found:
178              boundary = found.group(1)
179              continue
180          found = CASE_LINE.match(line)
181          if found:
182              cases.append((found.group(1), found.group(2)))
183              continue
184          found = ACTOR_LINE.match(line)
185          if found:
186              actors.append((found.group(1), found.group(2)))
187              continue
188          found = ASSOC_LINE.match(line)
189          if found:
190              assocs.append((found.group(1), found.group(2)))
191              continue
192          found = EXTEND_LINE.match(line)
193          if found:
194              extends.append((found.group(1), found.group(2), found.group(3)))
195      if not actors or not cases:
196          raise SystemExit("사람 그림으로 그릴 행위자나 유스케이스가 없습니다.")
197      return boundary, actors, cases, assocs, extends
198  
199  
200  def text_width(text: str) -> int:
201      return sum(17 if ord(char) > 127 else 9 for char in text)
202  
203  
204  def usecase_svg(source: str) -> str:
205      boundary, actors, cases, assocs, extends = parse_usecase(source)
206      parsed = [(case_id, BREAK_LINE.split(label)) for case_id, label in cases]
207      ellipse_w = max(max(text_width(line) for line in lines) for _, lines in parsed) + 52
208      row_h = 78
209      ellipse_h = 50
210      pad_top = 40
211      pad_x = 48
212      boundary_x = 188
213      boundary_y = 10
214      extend_room = 28
215      boundary_w = pad_x + ellipse_w + pad_x + extend_room
216      boundary_h = pad_top + row_h * len(parsed) + 8
217      ellipse_x = boundary_x + pad_x
218      centers: dict[str, float] = {}
219      for index, (case_id, _) in enumerate(parsed):
220          centers[case_id] = boundary_y + pad_top + index * row_h + ellipse_h / 2
221      linked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
222      for actor_id, case_id in assocs:
223          linked.setdefault(actor_id, []).append(case_id)
224  
225      def actor_anchor(actor_id: str) -> float:
226          targets = linked.get(actor_id, [])
227          if not targets:
228              return boundary_y + boundary_h / 2
229          return sum(centers[case_id] for case_id in targets) / len(targets)
230  
231      width = boundary_x + boundary_w + 20
232      height = boundary_y + boundary_h + 28
233      parts = [
234          f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="{width}" height="{height}" viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}">',
235          "<defs><marker id='extend-arrow' markerWidth='8' markerHeight='8' refX='7' refY='4' orient='auto'>",
236          "<path d='M0,0 L8,4 L0,8 Z' fill='#555'/></marker></defs>",
237          f"<rect x='{boundary_x}' y='{boundary_y}' width='{boundary_w}' height='{boundary_h}' fill='none' stroke='#9a9a9a' stroke-width='1.4'/>",
238          f"<text x='{boundary_x + boundary_w / 2}' y='{boundary_y + 24}' text-anchor='middle' font-size='15' fill='#333'>{html.escape(boundary)}</text>",
239      ]
240      hands: dict[str, tuple[float, float]] = {}
241      for index, (actor_id, name) in enumerate(actors):
242          center_x = 78
243          center_y = actor_anchor(actor_id)
244          radius = 13
245          head_y = center_y - 34
246          neck = head_y + radius
247          hip = neck + 30
248          arm_y = neck + 12
249          foot = hip + 24
250          arm = 20
251          leg = 15
252          parts.append(
253              f"<g fill='none' stroke='#222' stroke-width='1.7' stroke-linecap='round'>"
254              f"<circle cx='{center_x}' cy='{head_y}' r='{radius}'/>"
255              f"<line x1='{center_x}' y1='{neck}' x2='{center_x}' y2='{hip}'/>"
256              f"<line x1='{center_x - arm}' y1='{arm_y}' x2='{center_x + arm}' y2='{arm_y}'/>"
257              f"<line x1='{center_x}' y1='{hip}' x2='{center_x - leg}' y2='{foot}'/>"
258              f"<line x1='{center_x}' y1='{hip}' x2='{center_x + leg}' y2='{foot}'/>"
259              f"</g>"
260              f"<text x='{center_x}' y='{foot + 20}' text-anchor='middle' font-size='15' fill='#222'>{html.escape(name)}</text>"
261          )
262          hands[actor_id] = (center_x + arm, arm_y)
263      for actor_id, case_id in assocs:
264          start_x, start_y = hands[actor_id]
265          end_x = ellipse_x
266          end_y = centers[case_id]
267          bend = (start_x + end_x) / 2
268          parts.append(
269              f"<path d='M {start_x} {start_y} C {bend} {start_y}, {bend} {end_y}, {end_x} {end_y}' fill='none' stroke='#777' stroke-width='1.3'/>"
270          )
271      right = ellipse_x + ellipse_w
272      for source_id, label, target_id in extends:
273          start_y = centers[source_id]
274          end_y = centers[target_id]
275          lane = right + 16
276          mid_y = (start_y + end_y) / 2
277          parts.append(
278              f"<path d='M {right} {start_y} C {lane} {start_y}, {lane} {end_y}, {right} {end_y}' fill='none' stroke='#555' stroke-width='1.3' stroke-dasharray='4 3' marker-end='url(#extend-arrow)'/>"
279              f"<text x='{lane + 8}' y='{mid_y + 4}' font-size='13' fill='#333'>{html.escape(label)}</text>"
280          )
281      for case_id, lines in parsed:
282          center_y = centers[case_id]
283          parts.append(
284              f"<ellipse cx='{ellipse_x + ellipse_w / 2}' cy='{center_y}' rx='{ellipse_w / 2}' ry='{ellipse_h / 2}' fill='#f4f4f4' stroke='#666' stroke-width='1.3'/>"
285          )
286          if len(lines) == 1:
287              parts.append(
288                  f"<text x='{ellipse_x + ellipse_w / 2}' y='{center_y + 5}' text-anchor='middle' font-size='15' fill='#222'>{html.escape(lines[0])}</text>"
289              )
290          else:
291              first = center_y - 6
292              spans = "".join(
293                  f"<tspan x='{ellipse_x + ellipse_w / 2}' y='{first + offset * 18}'>{html.escape(line)}</tspan>"
294                  for offset, line in enumerate(lines)
295              )
296              parts.append(f"<text text-anchor='middle' font-size='15' fill='#222'>{spans}</text>")
297      parts.append("</svg>")
298      return "".join(parts)
299

301  def svg_page(svg: str) -> str:
302      return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
303  <html lang="ko">
304  <head>
305  <meta charset="utf-8" />
306  <style>
307    html, body {{ margin: 0; background: #fff; }}
308    body {{ padding: 20px; font-family: "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif; }}
309    svg {{ font-family: "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif; }}
310  </style>
311  </head>
312  <body>
313  {svg}
314  </body>
315  </html>
316  """

318  
319  def diagram_page(source: str) -> str:
320      if "actor-style: person" in source:
321          return svg_page(usecase_svg(source))
322      escaped = html.escape(source)
323      return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
324  <html lang="ko">
325  <head>
326  <meta charset="utf-8" />
327  <style>
328    html, body {{ margin: 0; background: #fff; }}
329    body {{ padding: 24px; }}
330    .mermaid {{ font-family: "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif; }}
331  </style>
332  </head>
333  <body>
334  <pre class="mermaid">{escaped}</pre>
335  <script type="module">
336    import mermaid from "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/mermaid@11/dist/mermaid.esm.min.mjs";
337    mermaid.initialize({{ startOnLoad: true, theme: "neutral", fontFamily: "Malgun Gothic, sans-serif" }});
338  </script>
339  </body>
340  </html>
341  """
342

344  def crop_whitespace(path: Path) -> None:
345      image = Image.open(path).convert("RGB")
346      background = Image.new("RGB", image.size, (255, 255, 255))
347      box = ImageChops.difference(image, background).getbbox()
348      if box is None:
349          raise SystemExit(f"차트가 비어 있습니다: {path.name}")
350      pad = 16
351      left = max(box[0] - pad, 0)
352      top = max(box[1] - pad, 0)
353      right = min(box[2] + pad, image.width)
354      bottom = min(box[3] + pad, image.height)
355      cropped = image.crop((left, top, right, bottom))
356      if cropped.width < 40 or cropped.height < 40:
357          raise SystemExit(f"차트가 너무 작습니다: {path.name}")
358      cropped.save(path)
359  
360  
361  def render_diagram(source: str) -> str:
362      digest = hashlib.sha256(source.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]
363      USED_DIAGRAMS.add(digest)
364      DIAGRAMS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
365      png = DIAGRAMS / f"{digest}.png"
366      if not png.is_file():
367          browser = find_browser()
368          if browser is None:
369              raise SystemExit("차트를 그릴 Edge 또는 Chrome을 찾지 못했습니다.")
370          html_path = DIAGRAMS / f"{digest}.html"
371          html_path.write_text(diagram_page(source), encoding="utf-8")
372          profile = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="specs-chart-"))
373          command = [
374              str(browser),
375              "--headless=new",
376              "--disable-gpu",
377              "--no-first-run",
378              "--hide-scrollbars",
379              "--force-device-scale-factor=2",
380              "--virtual-time-budget=12000",
381              "--window-size=1400,2200",
382              f"--user-data-dir={profile}",
383              f"--screenshot={png.resolve()}",
384              html_path.resolve().as_uri(),
385          ]
386          try:
387              subprocess.run(command, check=True, timeout=60)
388              deadline = time.time() + 8
389              while not png.is_file() and time.time() < deadline:
390                  time.sleep(0.2)
391          finally:
392              html_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
393              shutil.rmtree(profile, ignore_errors=True)
394          if not png.is_file():
395              raise SystemExit(f"차트 이미지가 만들어지지 않았습니다: {png.name}")
396          crop_whitespace(png)
397      relative = png.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
398      return f'<figure class="chart"><img src="{html.escape(relative)}" alt="차트" /></figure>'
399  
400  
401  def cleanup_diagrams() -> None:
402      if not DIAGRAMS.is_dir():
403          return
404      for path in DIAGRAMS.iterdir():
405          if path.is_dir():
406              shutil.rmtree(path, ignore_errors=True)
407          elif path.stem not in USED_DIAGRAMS:
408              path.unlink()
409  
410  
411  def write_pdf(html_path: Path, pdf_path: Path) -> None:
412      browser = find_browser()
413      if browser is None:
414          raise SystemExit("PDF로 인쇄할 Edge 또는 Chrome을 찾지 못했습니다.")
415      if pdf_path.exists():
416          pdf_path.unlink()
417      profile = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="specs-pdf-"))
418      command = [
419          str(browser),
420          "--headless=new",
421          "--disable-gpu",
422          "--no-first-run",
423          "--no-pdf-header-footer",
424          f"--user-data-dir={profile}",
425          f"--print-to-pdf={pdf_path.resolve()}",
426          html_path.resolve().as_uri(),
427      ]
428      try:
429          subprocess.run(command, check=True, timeout=120)
430      finally:
431          shutil.rmtree(profile, ignore_errors=True)
432      if not pdf_path.is_file() or pdf_path.stat().st_size < 1000:
433          raise SystemExit(f"PDF가 만들어지지 않았습니다: {pdf_path}")
434  
435  
436  def main() -> None:
437      markdown = combined_markdown()
438      MD_PATH.write_text(markdown, encoding="utf-8")
439      HTML_PATH.write_text(render_html(markdown_to_html(markdown)), encoding="utf-8")
440      cleanup_diagrams()
441      write_pdf(HTML_PATH, PDF_PATH)
442      print(f"Wrote {MD_PATH}")
443      print(f"Wrote {HTML_PATH}")
444      print(f"Wrote {PDF_PATH} ({PDF_PATH.stat().st_size // 1024} KB)")
445  
446  
447  if __name__ == "__main__":
448      main()
